[EVENT: YEM79387]
Date: 2023-03-05
Country: Yemen | Location: Az Zihar
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Yemen) [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Al Masdar; Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Mareb Press; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 5 March 2023, unidentified gunmen fired shots with a silencer inside a meeting room of Houthi political officials in As Said Educational Complex in Az Zihar (Al Dhihar, Ibb) where it injured the secretary-general of the Yemeni Football Association. The meeting was also attended by the director-general of the Education Office as well as the Houthi under-secretary for Ibb governorate. One source speculated that the latter -- Abd al-Fattah Ghallab -- was the one targeted. The victim later accused the Houthi-affiliated deputy director of the Education Office in Jiblah district of being behind the assassination attempt.

---

[EVENT: YEM79402]
Date: 2023-03-06
Country: Yemen | Location: Aden - Aden International Airport
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 6 March 2023, teachers and students from the Academy of Civil Aviation and Engineering held a protest in front of Aden - Aden International Airport (Khur Maksar) to denounce the stop put to the construction of a hangar for the academy that was started 5 months ago which prevented students from completing their curriculum. Protesters set up a tent for a permanent sit-in after they called on the Saudi crown prince to intervene to rebuild the hangar with no response to their call.

[EVENT: YEM79583]
Date: 2023-03-08
Country: Yemen | Location: Shamlan
Type: Strategic developments > Looting/property destruction | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
Property destruction: On 8 March 2023, Houthi forces led by the Houthi security supervisor of Hamdan district 'assaulted' dozens of workers from the Shamlan Mineral Water Factory and demolished parts of it in Shamlan (Hamdan, Sanaa). They also closed it, and besieged it having set up a tent for gunmen in front of the factory's door - until at least 14 March 2023 - to prevent the entry and the exit of employees and trucks. One source claimed that the attack was due to the refusal of factory owners to share the factory's revenues with a Houthi figure. The Houthi supervisor and his forces had stormed the same factory the previous year (coded as a separate event).

[EVENT: YEM79623]

Country: Yemen | Location: Sanaa
Type: Strategic developments > Agreement | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council [State forces]
Actor2: Government of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: 26 September News - IRG; Aden al Ghad; ICRC; Reuters; Twitter; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 20 March 2023, Houthi de facto authorities and the IRG agreed on an exchange of 887 detainees including 181 in Houthi prisons and 706 in IRG prisons (coded to capital Sanaa) (Amanat al Asimah). Houthi-held detainees included 4 journalists sentenced to death, the former IRG minister of defense, the brother of former IRG president Hadi, a son and a brother of PLC member Tariq Saleh, 4 sons of former vice president Ali Mohsen, and 18 coalition soldiers (15 Saudis & 3 Sudanese). 13 Houthi detainees were exchanged for 1 Saudi prisoner on 8 April and 973 detainees were released over 14-17 April in multiple operations facilitated by the ICRC. A further 77 detainees were released on 20 April.

The gunmen also looted the house and the adjacent qat farm belonging to the same owner. The reason behind the incident was not reported but the owner of the house and his son were abducted by Houthi security forces the month prior.

---

Around 31 March 2023 (as reported), an unidentified armed group tried to assassinate a judge (likely via shooting) while he left the courthouse of Jabal Habashy district (coded to district center Yufrus) (Taizz). [EVENT: YEM110716]
Date: 2023-03-31
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Marir
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: West Coast Joint Forces [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 31 March 2023, Houthi forces clashed with the WCJF, including the 4th National Resistance Brigade, in the Wadi Al Mareer area (coded to Al Marir) (Jabal Ras, Al Hudaydah). During the clashes, 3 explosives-laden Houthi drones targeted WCJF positions. The clashes took place over the period of 27, 28 and 31 March. 1 WCJF soldier from the 4th National Resistance Brigade was killed and 4 others were wounded. Fatalities split across 3 events. 0 fatality coded to this event.
